Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The modules variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 624 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 369 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_624(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 624. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^369(S, F) is trivial. QED.
